2. Introduction
SmartGraph COM is a n ActiveX com pone nt that come s a s DLL to be integrate d in a n y ActiveX capabl e
environ ment. Beside the g eneral pro g ra mming i n terfa c e s to Active X ena bled en vironme n ts, SmartGraph CO M
feature s som e gen eral communi catio n s inte rfa c e s to the PC s ha rd wa re, netwo rk o r simpl e digit a l I/O
s u bs ys tems .
SmartGraph COM ne ed s the Sma r tGraph appli c ati on run n ing t o c a r r y o u t variou s ope rations on te mplate
databa se s off line an d onlin e on the Allte c La se r sy ste m s. It can co ntrol the L a se r ope ratio n in cludi ng start and
stop of Laser marking. Wi th its c apability to create new template gra phi cs f r om basic te m p late language,
allowin g to i n spe c t a nd m o d if y t h e t e m p late u s ing S m artG raph s use r inte rfa c e , inclu d ing se tting of prede fine
variable s , SmartGraph CO M is an ideal comp one nt for Laser inte gration into flexible indu strial environ ment s.
T h e f o llo w ing docum ent is a comp reh e n sive u s er s manual o n the SmartG ra phCOM interf ace met hod s. It
descri b e s all obje c t metho d s, their pa ra meters and return code s.
Along with the example code provided by Alltec, this allows for i ndep ende nt developm ent and integ r ati on of
Alltec Lasers into cu s t o m e r spe c if ic industrial control environment s.
The follo wing grap hics illu strate s the g eneral a r chitectu re fo r Allte c La se r integ r ation u s in g SmartGraph COM .
This co mpo n ent interact s with corre s p o nding layer in the Sma r tGraph appli c ati on whi c h in turn i s co nne cted to
the lase r marking system.
The follo wing docu m ent wi ll descri be ho w to user the SmartGraph COM inte rfa c e and expl ain the return co de s
from variou s comm uni cati on layers

Public Sub CreateParameterIndices()
 Dim lRet As Long
 Dim I As Integer
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'create SmartGraphCOM instance
 lRet = sg.Connect() 'connect to SmartGraph
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\Standard.ydb") 'connect to selected offline database
 lRet = sg.CreateParameterSet("MyParameter2", "Paper")
 For I = 2 To 10 'currently there is a maximum of 10 index levels per parameter set
 lRet = sg.CreateParameterIndex("MyParameter2", 1)
 Next I
 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component
End Sub
As a result, the pa ram e ter dialog win d o w sho w s a total of 10 in dex levels fo r the n e w pa ramete r set. All

sgcCommandError -20002 This error, generated by SmartGraph indicates an error in the received command, such as mismatch in number of parameters.
If versions of SmartGraph and SmartGraphCOM match, this error indicates software malfunction or reception of corrupted
data.

sgcObjectSizeError -20036 Error when resizing an object. Possible causes: Specified size out of range.

Example
The following VB procedure attaches to SmartGraph, creates a new template, inserts some objects and rotates them. In its user interface the sample program displays the retrieved geometryettings.
P ublic Sub GetGeometry() Dim lRet As Long
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph Dim dblX As Double
 Dim dblY As Double Dim dblWidth As Double
 Dim dblHeight As Double Dim dblAngle As Double
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'create SmartGraphCOM instance lRet = sg.Connect() 'connect to SmartGraph
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\Standard.ydb") 'connect to offline database lRet = sg.CreateTemplate("MyTemplate7", "Paper", "Allprint", True)
 lRet = sg.SetTemplateText("MyTemplate7", "Arc(5,5,0,360,ARC_CLOSE, 5,5)" & _ "Rect(10,10:10:10:0)" & _
 "Abs(-10,-10) ""A"" ")
 lRet = sg.GroupSelectedObjects() lRet = sg.SetRotation(30)
 lRet = sg.GetGeometry(dblX, dblY, dblWidth, dblHeight, dblAngle) With SmartGraphCOM_SamplesForm.GeometryListBox
 .Clear .AddItem "X-Pos: " & vbTab & dblX & " mm"
 .AddItem "Y-Pos: " & vbTab & dblY & " mm" .AddItem "Width: " & vbTab & dblWidth & " mm"
 .AddItem "Height: " & vbTab & dblHeight & " mm" .AddItem "Rot.: " & vbTab & dblAngle & " deg"
 End With lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component

Public Sub GetParameterIndex()

 Dim strParameterSetName As String

Remar ks
Exported tem p lates (*.tpl) contain the ref e ren c e d pa ra mete r set and the configu r a t ion (produ ct regi stratio n ) a s
well a s all ref e ren c e d fonts and logo s. These additio n a l obje c ts will be automatically imported along with a
template.
Specifics
(non e)
Example
The follo wing Visual Ba si c cod e conn e c ts to Sm art G rap h a nd i m port s a te mplate, a p a r amete r set and a
config uratio n (produ ct regi stration) into a new d a taba se.
Public Sub ImportDBObjectTest()
 Dim lRet As Long
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Dim strTemplateName As String
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph
 lRet = sg.Connect("")
lRet = sg.ImportDBObject(sgcTemplate, "Alltec_NEW", _
 "c:\dbobjects\MyTemplate.tpl", True)
lRet = sg.ImportDBObject(sgcParameterSet, "Paper_NEW",_
"c:\dbobjects\MyParameter.par", True)
lRet = sg.ImportDBObject(sgcConfiguration, "Allprint_NEW", _
 "c:\dbobjects\MyConfiguration.prd", True)
 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component

Example
Alltec_BW.jp g is a JPEG file contai nin g a black&white versio n of the A lltec/VideoJet log o . The dimen s ion of
the logo is 20 62 by 6 45 pix e ls. Th e follo wing Vi su al Basi c code co nne cts to Sm artGraph and import s thi s l ogo
(Alltec_B W.jp g) thre e time s: a) si ze limi t ed by 30x 15 0 mm b) size limited by 150x30 mm a nd c) assu ming a
image re sol u tion of 600 dpi . The s e l ogo s a r e pla c e d into a templa te alom ng wit h three re cta ngle s of 30x 150
mm, 150x30 mmm and the pre-cal c ul ate d size of the 600d pi image (88x28 mm ).
As can b e seen, the first logo is limited in hori z o n t al dimensi o n and the se cond in vertical dimen s ion. Both
without chan ging the a s pe ct ratio.
Public Sub PixelTraceTest()
 Dim lRet As Long
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph
 lRet = sg.Connect()
 sg.m_iCommandTimeout = 20
 lRet = sg.ImportTrace("Alltec_30x150", "-F PIXELTRACE -SIZE 30.000 150.00", "C:\Alltec_BW.jpg")
 lRet = sg.ImportTrace("Alltec_150x30", "-F PIXELTRACE -SIZE 150.000 30.00", "C:\Alltec_BW.jpg")
 lRet = sg.ImportTrace("Alltec_600dpi", "-F PIXELTRACE -DPI 600", "C:\Alltec_BW.jpg")
 lRet = sg.SetTemplateText("MyTemplate",
"Rect(-100,-75:30:150:0) Abs(-100,-5) Logo(""Alltec_30x150"") ")
 lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate",
"Rect(-70,0:150:30:0) Abs(-40,0) Logo(""Alltec_150x30"")")
 lRet = sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate",
"Rect(0,-80:88:28:0) Abs(0,-80) Logo(""Alltec_600dpi"")")
 lRet = sg.Disconnect
 Set sg = Nothing
End Sub

Description Syntax S ettings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See A lso
Description
T he ProtocolHub method sends a preformtted commandstring (output from another instance of
od allows for daisy chaining SmartGraphSmartGraph. This methods tunnels the protocol traffic between (another) SmartGraphCOM application and COM applicationsthat share a single instance of
the SmartGraph server, prviding relais capabilities to the actual SmartGraphCOM application.
Syntax
object.ProtocolHub( strCommand, strResponse)
The ProtocolHub method syntax has these arguments:
Part Description
SmartGraphCOM to the SmartGraph application and This methretrieves the response string from SmartGraph.
objectRequired. An object expression that evaluates to an object in the Applies To list.
strCommandRequired. String expression . The command string as received from another SmartGraphCOM application. This string will be sent to the SmartGraph
strResponse Required. String variable. The response string (including status or error codes, data and other) as generated by SmartGraph as a resut / response to the command string.

Example
The follo win g Visual Basi c code con n e c ts to SmartGrap h , create s a ne w conf iguratio n na med
MyCo n figura t ion2 an d sets the in dividual co nfigurati on p a rame ters to som e value s , that ca n be vi su ally
recogni ze d very easily. Th ese setting are not nec essarily useful in real life appli c ations.
Public Sub SetConfigurationValues()
 Dim lRet As Long
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'create SmartGraphCOM instance
 lRet = sg.Connect() 'connect to SmartGraph
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\Standard.ydb") 'connect to selected offline database
 lRet = sg.CreateConfiguration("MyConfiguration2")
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStartDelay, 1111)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcLockDelay, 2222)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStopDelay, 3333)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStartDist, 4444444)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcLockDist, 5555555)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStopDist, 6666666)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcProductMovement, sgcProductMoveEncoder)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcProductAngle, 222222)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcProductSpeed, 7777777)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcEncoderPulsesPerRotation, 8888)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcEncoderDistancePerRotation, 999999)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcEncoderRotationDirection, sgcEncoderLeft)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStartTrigger, sgcTriggerMode1Raise)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcConsTrigger, sgcTriggerMode2Fall)
 lRet = sg.SetConfigurationValue("MyConfiguration2", sgcStopTrigger, sgcTriggerMode3Raise)
 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component
End Sub
As a re sult, the config uratio n dialog wind ow sh o w s the new settings for MyConfig uration 2 .

Public Sub SetConfigurationAssignment()
 Dim lRet As Long
 Dim sg As CSmartGraph
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph 'create SmartGraphCOM instance
 lRet = sg.Connect() 'connect to SmartGraph

 lRet = sg.CreateConfiguration("MyConfiguration3")

 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component
End Sub

The followi n g snip pet o f VB code assume s the existen c e of a database d.ydb with a template
SetVarList Cle a rTe s t h a vin g thre e va ria b les MyPro mpt1 th rou g h MyPrompt 3  of type Extern T e xt defi ned.
The default value s of those variable s a r e sho w n o n the followi ng p icture:
Prompt varia b les sho w ing their default value s
T h e V B c o de first set s the s e three vari a b les to n e w v a lue s : ab c , def and ghi  witho u t appl ying them to the
t e m p la t e . T h an the metho d SetVarLi st Clea r i s calle d and on e of the varia b le s (MyPrompt2 ) is set to the string
123 . The s e setting s are a pplied to the template.
Public Sub SetVarListClearTest()
D i m s g A s C S m a r t G r a p h
 Dim lRet As Long
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph
 lRet = sg.Connect()
 lRet = sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\d.ydb")
 lRet = sg.SetActualTemplate("SetVarListClearTest")
 lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcPromptType, "MyPrompt1", "abc")
 lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcPromptType, "MyPrompt2", "def")
 lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcPromptType, "MyPrompt3", "ghi")
 lRet = sg.SetVarListClear
 lRet = sg.SetVarList(sgcPromptType, "MyPrompt2", "123")
 lRet = sg.SetVarListApply(sgcOffLine, "SetVarListClearTest")
 lRet = sg.Disconnect() 'disconnect from SmartGraph
 Set sg = Nothing 'clean up ActiveX Component
End Sub

 Dim oDOM As DOMDocument
 Dim oDB As DOMDocument
Dim strXMLFileName As String
Dim iOnOffline As Integer
 iOnOffline = sgcOffline
 strXMLFileName = "C:\SmartGrapCOM_Test.xml"
 Set sg = New CSmartGraph
 sg.m_iConnectTimeout = 300
 sg.m_iLaserConnectTimeout = 300
 sg.m_iCommandTimeout = 300
 lRet = sg.Connect()
 Set oDOM = New DOMDocument
 Set oDB = New DOMDocument
 lRet = sg.XML_GetDatabase(iOnOffline, strXML)
 If lRet = sgcOK Then
 oDB.loadXML (strXML)
 oDOM.appendChild oDB.documentElement
 End If
 oDOM.save (strXMLFileName)
 Browser.Show

79.13. Prompt Command
Description Syntax Settings Return Values Remarks Specifics Example Applies To See Also
Description
Command template language command defines a text variable to be marked and which can be et by the SetVar
The Prompt
s or SetVarList method.
yntax
ength, strDefaultText, strName, iPromptOnce )
The Prompt command syntax has these arguments:
art Description
S
Prompt( iL
P
iLengthRequired. Numeric expression. Specifies the length of the text variable in number of characters.
strDefaultText Required. String expression. Sets the default text to be marked if the variable has not been changed (set) by any SetVar
 or SetVarList method call.
strName Required. String expression. Specifies the name of the variable. This name will be references by any SetVar
 or SetVarList method calls.
iPromptOnce Required. Numeric expression. Specifies marked more than once or not. See if any content of the variable may be Settings
 for further information.
Settings
The iPromptOnce parameter has following settings:
Constant Value Description
sgcPromptOnce 1 Content of the variable can be marked once only. Prior to next marking of the template the variable has to changed to a new value using the
SetVar
 or SetVarList method.
sgcPromptMultiple 0 Content of the variable can be marked more than once.
Return Values

80.5. HTML JavaScript
The JavaScript integration is one the most simple development environment or other large ActiveX enabl(client side) integration that does not require any ed packages such as Office. Just an ActiveX enabled
browser like Microsoft Internet Explorer. Make sure the browser is enabled for AxtiveX and the security settings of the browser allow for AxtiveX
execution.
In order to write a first Hello World use any text editor and enter following lines of code:
<html> <head>
<meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=windows-1252"> <title>Hello World</title>
</head> <body>
<script language=javascript> var sg;
 sg = new ActiveXObject("SmartGraphCOM.CSmartGraph"); sg.Connect("","","");
 sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\\Standard.ydb"); sg.CreateTemplate("MyTemplate","Paper","Allprint",true);
 sg.AddTemplateText("MyTemplate", " Abs(-30,0) Height(8) \"Hello World\" "); sg.Disconnect();
</script> </body>
</html>
and save the files as HelloWorld.htm
The gray colored code is needed for providing a syntaprocessed by the browser. This is standard for all ctical correct HTML page in order to be properly web pages. Only the few red lines of code are needed to
establish a first Hello World application.

80.6. PERL
The PERL integration is a very simple method of integration. As a precondition the PERL interpreter environment must be set up in Windows environment.
All that is needed is a text editor to write following lines of code into a new file named HelloWorld.pl
use strict; use Win32::OLE;
my $sg = Win32::OLE->new("SmartGraphCOM.CSmartGraph"); my $lRet;
$lRet = $sg->Connect(); $lRet = $sg->CreateTemplate("MyTemplate", "Paper", "Allprint", 1);
$lRet = $sg->SetTemplateText("MyTemplate", "Abs(-30,0) Height(8) \"Hello World!\" "); $lRet = $sg->Disconnect();
The gray colored code is needed to enable ActiveX support to the PERL interpreter.
Please note that this examples assumes that a SmartGrroot directory of the C-drive. This path can be chanaph database named Standard.ydb is located in the ged to any other location that specifies a database. Upon
SmartGraph installation (setup) a basic database directory. This databse could be copied of the named Standard.ydb is placed into the installation path could be changed to point to that location.
Start SmartGraph (which always must be running in order to use SmartGraphCOM functions) and run the PERL script file by double-clicking (in explorer or other file shell tool). The SmartGraph template editor
window shows the result as follows:

80.7. TCL
The TCL integration is a very simple method of integration. As a precondition the TCL interpreter v8.1.x or higher environment is required and must be set up in Windows
environment.
All that is needed is a text editor to write following lines of code into a new file named HelloWorld.tcl
package require tcom
set sg [::tcom::ref createobject "SmartGraphCOM.CSmartGraph"] set lRet [$sg Connect]
set lRet [$sg ConnectOfflineDB "C:\\standard.ydb"] set lRet [$sg CreateTemplate "MyTemplate" "Paper" "Allprint" 1]
set lRet [$sg SetTemplateText "MyTemplate" "Abs(-30,0) Height(8) \"Hello World!\" "] set lRet [$sg Disconnect]
The gray colored code is needed to enable ActiveX support to the TCL interpreter.
Please note that this examples assumes that a SmartGrroot directory of the C-drive. This path can be chanaph database named Standard.ydb is located in the ged to any other location that specifies a database. Upon
SmartGraph installation (setup) a basic database directory. This databse could be copied of the named Standard.ydb is placed into the installation path could be changed to point to that location.
Start SmartGraph (which always must be running in order to use SmartGraphCOM functions) and run the TCL script file by double-clicking (in explorer or other file shell tool). The SmartGraph template editor window
shows the result as follows:

var sg; sg = new ActiveXObject("SmartGraphCOM.CSmartGraph");
sg.Connect("","",""); sg.ConnectOfflineDB("c:\\standard.ydb");
sg.CreateTemplate("HelloWorld","Paper","Allprint",true); sg.AddTemplateText("HelloWorld", "Abs(-30,0) Height(8) \"Hello World\" ");
sg.Disconnect();
